Let's design robust custom optimizer generic:

```
def conv_max_and_weights(x, fft_len, tau=None, active_tol=...):
    F=np.fft.rfft(x, fft_len)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, fft_len)[:2*m-1]
    # clip small neg
    np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
    mmax=c.max()
    if tau: p=exp((c-mmax)/tau)/sum
    else p not
    return c, mmax, p
```

Gradient h from p and x:
```
def conv_h(x,p,fft_len,m):
    pR=np.zeros(fft_len); pR[:2*m-1]=p[::-1]
    Fx=rfft(x,fft_len); Fp=rfft(pR,fft_len)
    conv=irfft(Fx*Fp, fft_len)
    h=conv[m-1:2*m-1][::-1]
    # 2*h? We can omit factor 2; direction same scale 1/2.
    return h
```

def proj_simplex(a, step_d):
    a_new = a + step_d  # step_d already scaled by lr
    a_new = np.maximum(a_new,0)
    s=a_new.sum()
    if s<eps: return a,0
    return a_new/s, s?
```

Objective function:
```
def make_lu(m, tau, ridge=1e-12):
  fft_len...
  def fun(z):
     zc = z - np.mean(z)
     # maybe clip zc to [-50,50]
     zc = np.clip(zc, -60, 60)
     a = np.exp(zc - zc.max()); a /= a.sum()
     F = np.fft.rfft(a, fft_len); c=irfft(F*F, fft_len)[:2*m-1]; c=np.maximum(c,0)
     maxc = c.max()
     # logsumexp stable:
     s = np.expm1(c.max()-maxc)??
     Fval = maxc + tau * np.log(np.exp((c-maxc)/tau).sum())
     Fval += ridge * np.dot(zc,zc)
     return Fval
  def fun_grad(z):
     same compute c,p, maxc, Z
     # weights p = exp((c-maxc)/tau)/Z
     h = conv_h(a,p)/tau? Actually grad_F w.r.t a = 2/tau * h? Let's derive: F = maxc + tau log Z. dF/dc = exp((c-maxc)/tau)/Z = p/tau? Wait p = exp((c-maxc)/tau)/sum exp = e/ Z. Then dF/dc = e/ (tau Z) = p/tau. So grad_a = 2 sum (p/tau) ... = (2/tau)h. Then grad_z = a*(grad_a - mean(grad_a)).
     g = (2/tau)*h
     g = g - np.mean(g)
     gz = a*g
     gz = gz - np.mean(gz) # should be 0
     gz += 2*ridge*zc
     return Fval, gz
```
Need ensure Fval name conflict with F FFT; use val.

L-BFGS can call fun_grad with z vector. For m=4806, z length 4806, zc clipping: if z range large, clipping changes objective non-smooth. We can avoid clipping by using stable softmax with zc - max; if zc values > 700 overflow? We can subtract max. Underflow to zero for very negative okay; gradient for those a=0, but if zc clipped not. We can just subtract max; no clipping needed for exp if we do zc - zmax, all <=0, exp safe. So:
```
z0 = z - z.mean()
z0 -= z0.max()
a = exp(z0); a /= sum(a)
```
This is softmax with mean-zero shifted; adding max shift doesn't change a? z - mean - max(z-mean) is same as z - max(z) actually because max(z-mean)=max(z)-mean. Softmax invariant to shift; we can just a = exp(z - max(z)). But to remove flat direction, if we ignore mean, L-BFGS can still change max. Ridge on zc before shifting. Need zc for ridge; after shifting for exp.

Ridge: zc = z - mean(z). For gradient, ridge adds 2r zc. But objective uses a=softmax(z) (same as softmax(zc)); val = F(a)+ r*||zc||^2. Good.

However, even with ridge, the direction adding constant has gradient 2r constant, not zero, L-BFGS step will alter mean to reduce ridge, effectively fixing a. It may spend iterations but okay. Fast conv:
```
def fast_conv(a, fft_len):
   F = np.fft.rfft(a, fft_len)
   c = np.fft.irfft(F*F, fft_len, n=fft_len)
   c = c[:2*m-1]
   np.maximum(c, 0, out=c)
   return c
```
Need if F*F large? a sum1, values <=1, F values O(1), fine. For n large maybe.

Gradient h:
```
def grad_h(a, p, fft_len):
   pr = np.zeros(fft_len)
   pr[:2*m-1] = p[::-1]
   Fx = np.fft.rfft(a, fft_len)
   Fp = np.fft.rfft(pr, fft_len)
   conv = np.fft.irfft(Fx*Fp, fft_len)
   h = conv[m-1:2*m-1][::-1]
   # h length m? m-1 to 2m-2 inclusive = m elements.
   return h
```
Double check length: conv length fft_len; segment [m-1,2m-1) length m. Good.

For L-BFGS objective, we need compute c and h. We can compute Fx once. p from c. h = conv of a with reversed p. We can avoid second rfft of p? Need Fp. So 3 rfft? def _nfft(m):
    l=2*m-1
    p=1
    while p<l: p <<=1
    return p

class _ConvCtx:
   def __init__(self,m):
      self.m=m; self.ell=_nfft(m); self.len=2*m-1; self.flen=ell
   def conv(self,a): ...
   def score(self,a): return 2*m*max(conv(a))
   def softmax_obj(self,z,tau,r=1e-10): returns val, grad

`softmax_obj` details:
```
def obj(z):
   zc = z - z.mean()
   zc = np.where(np.isfinite(zc), zc, 0)
   a = np.exp(zc - zc.max())  # if zc.max inf? no
   sa = a.sum()
   if sa <=0 or not finite: a=np.ones(m)/m
   else a/=sa
   c=conv(a)
   mc=c.max()
   if not finite: return 1e100, zeros(m) maybe
   shifted=(c-mc)/tau
   # to avoid overflow if tau small? shifted<=0
   ew=np.exp(shifted)
   Z=ew.sum()
   val = mc + tau*np.log(Z) + ridge*np.dot(zc,zc)
   # grad
   p=ew/Z
   h=grad_h(a,p)
   g = (2/tau)*h
   gmean = g.mean()
   gz = a*(g - gmean)
   gz += 2*ridge*zc
   gz -= gz.mean()  # ensure zero? plus ridge mean? Actually if gz mean not zero due to a*gmean? Let's compute properly.
```

def _normalize(a):
   a=np.maximum(a,0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<1e-15: a=np.ones(m)/m
   else: a=a/s
   return a

def _smooth_random(m, rng, amp, blocks):
   # random p with low-frequency cos
   z = np.zeros(m)
   for freq in rng.choice? Use np.fft.rfft random
   x = rng.normal(size=m)
   # lowpass: take rfft, zero high freq
   F = np.fft.rfft(x)
   F[max(len(F)//2:)] = 0? keep low.
   x = np.fft.irfft(F, m)
   a = 1 + amp*x/x.std()
   a = np.exp(amp*x) maybe
   a = np.maximum(a,0)
```
Simpler: random `u = np.linspace(0,1,m)`, `z = amp1*np.sin(2π f1 u + ph1) + amp2*np.sin(... )`, a=1+z, clip. Or `a = np.exp(amp * rng.normal(m))`; not smooth. Use random Fourier low frequencies:
```
def random_lowpass(m, rng, kmax, amp):
   F=np.zeros(m//2+1, complex)
   ks = rng.choice(np.arange(1,kmax+1), size=min(3,kmax), replace=False)
   for k in ks:
      c = rng.normal(scale=1) + 1j*rng.normal(scale=1)
      F[k] = c
      if m-k>=0 and m-k < len(F): F[m-k] = np.conj(c)
   x = np.fft.irfft(F, m) * np.sqrt(2.0/m)
   if x.std()>0: x=x/x.std()
   a = np.exp(amp*x)
   a = np.maximum(a,1e-6)
   a/=a.sum()
```
This yields smooth (few freq). Using random Gaussian transform? Good.

Parent downsample:
```
def downsample(a,n_to_m):
   m_target; if len a <= m: upsample; else:
      bins = np.floor(np.arange(len(a))*m/len(a)).astype(int)
      sums=np.bincount(bins, weights=a, minlength=m)
      counts=np.bincount(bins, minlength=m).astype(float)
      out=sums/np.maximum(counts,1)
      out/=out.sum()
```

Let's write `_refine_with_lbbox(a0, m, time_limit, stages, maxfun_per_stage, exp_bound=60)` robust.

```
def _refine_with_lbbox(a0, time_limit, start_time=0):
    a = _pos_norm(a0)
    ctx=...
    z0 = np.log(a + 1e-12/n)
    z0 = z0 - z0.mean()
    best_a=a.copy(); best_s=fast_score(a)
    # maybe compute current max
    maxc0 = fast_conv(a).max()
    for i, frac in enumerate(tau_fractions):
        if time.monotonic() >= start_time+time_limit: break
        mc = maxc0 if first else fast_conv(a).max()
        tau = max(mc*frac, 1e-12*max(mc,1e-12))
        def fun(z, tau=tau):
           zc = z - z.mean()
           # if any nonfinite, reset
           if not np.all(np.isfinite(zc)): return 1e100, np.zeros_like(z)
           zexp = zc - zc.max()
           e = np.exp(zexp)
           s = e.sum()
           if not finite s or s<=0: ...
           aa = e/s
           c = conv(aa)
           mc = c.max()
           if not finite mc: return 1e100,...
           sh = (c-mc)/tau
           # If tau very small and sh min < -745, exp underflows fine; no overflow.
           ew = np.exp(sh)
           Z = ew.sum()
           if Z <=0 or not finite: # fallback p uniform over max
               p = (c == mc).astype(float); p/=p.sum()
               logZ = 0
           else:
               p=ew/Z; logZ=np.log(Z)
           val=mc + tau*logZ + ridge*np.dot(zc,zc)
           h=grad_h(aa,p)
           grad_a = 2.0*h/tau
           gz = aa*grad_a - aa*np.dot(aa,grad_a) + 2*ridge*zc
           if not finite gz: gz[:] =0
           gz -= gz.mean()
           return val, gz
        res = minimize(fun, z, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=False, bounds=bounds, options={...})
        if finite res.x: z=res.x - res.x.mean()
        a = softmax(z)
        sc=fast_score(a)
        if sc < best_s: best...
    return best_a,best_s
```
Need `z` updated across stages. If minimize fails and z unchanged, okay.

Set `bounds` as tuple of tuples? For large m, maybe `bounds = [(-exp_bound, exp_bound)] * m`. L-BFGS-B may convert to array. It's okay. For m=4806 memory small.

`options`: Use `maxiter=20`, `maxfun=maxfun_per_stage`, `ftol=1e-9`, `gtol=1e-4`, `maxls=20`. Because objective scale ~1/m, gtol 1e-4 may be too loose? Gradient components ~1/m ~2e-4, norm ~0.04. gtol default 1e-5 maybe. Use `gtol=1e-5`. For stages, we may want not early stop too much; set maxiter=10? Let's refine low starts.

Downsample parent to low m:
```
def _downsample(a,m):
    n=len(a)
    if n==m: return a.copy()
    if n<m: return _upsample(a,m)
    idx=np.floor(np.arange(n)*m/n).astype(np.int64)
    idx=np.minimum(idx,m-1)
    sums=np.bincount(idx, weights=a, minlength=m)
    counts=np.bincount(idx, minlength=m)
    out=sums/np.maximum(counts,1)
    return _pos_norm(out)
```
If parent has zeros, out may zeros; add eps.

Upsample:
```
def _upsample(a,n):
   m=len(a)
   if m==n: return a.copy()
   if m<n:
      xo=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m) if m>1 else [0]
      xn=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
      out=np.interp(xn,xo,a)
   else: out=_downsample(a,n)
   return _pos_norm(out)
```
If m=1? not.

`_pos_norm(a, eps=1e-12)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,float)
a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0)
a=np.maximum(a,0)
# add tiny floor if needed? For initialization, add eps = 1e-12/max(len,1)
a = a + eps
s=a.sum()
if s < 0: fallback uniform
return a/s
```
For final return, don't need add eps if sum okay. For optimization, add small floor to make log.

Random lowpass:
```
def _rand_lowpass(m, rng, kmax=None, amps=(0.3,0.7)):
   kmax = max(2, min(8, m//4))
   F=np.zeros(m//2+1, dtype=complex)
   ks = rng.choice(np.arange(1,kmax+1), size=min(3,kmax), replace=False)
   for k in ks:
       amp = rng.uniform(0.3,1.0)
       phase = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       val = amp*np.exp(1j*phase)
       F[k]=val
       if m-k >=0 and m-k < len(F): F[m-k]=np.conj(val)
   x=np.fft.irfft(F,m)
   x=(x-x.mean())
   sd=x.std()
   if sd>1e-12: x=x/sd
   amp=rng.uniform(0.3,0.8)
   a=np.exp(amp*x)
   return _pos_norm(a,1e-9)
```
Using exp(amp*x) with x std1, amp up to0.8, positive. Good. For m=256, kmax maybe8. For full random, kmax=4 maybe smooth.

Two-block:
```
def _two_block(m,rng):
   p=rng.uniform(0.3,0.7)
   alpha=rng.uniform(0.25,0.75)
   k=int(round(p*m))
   a=np.empty(m)
   a[:k]=alpha/k
   a[k:]=(1-alpha)/(m-k)
   return _pos_norm(a)
```
Maybe use a smooth ramp instead of hard block:
```
u=np.linspace(-1,1,m); a=0.5 + s*u; a=np.exp?```
Add parametric starts:
```
def _linear_base(m,s):
   u=np.linspace(-1,1,m)
   a=0.5 + s*u
   if min<=0: clip or choose s smaller.
   return _pos_norm(np.maximum(a,1e-6))
```
s in [0.05,0.15,0.25]. This is simple.

Could include "wiggly" two-sided mass: a=1+0.5 sin(2π i/m + phase) positive? sin min -1, 1-0.5 positive; good. But random lowpass covers.

```
import time, math
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_CTX_CACHE = {}

def _new_ctx(m):
    key=m
    if key in _CTX_CACHE: return _CTX_CACHE[key]
    need=2*m-1
    p=1
    while p<need: p <<=1
    ctx={'m':m,'fft':p,'outlen':need}
    _CTX_CACHE[key]=ctx
    return ctx
```
Since propose_candidate may be called once, global okay. Could be called multiple? Use local dict? The harness maybe once. Global okay. But if multiple calls, cache persists; fine.

`_conv(a)`:
```
def _conv(a):
    m=a.size
    ctx=_ctx(m); f=ctx['fft']; L=ctx['outlen']
    F=np.fft.rfft(a, f)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, f)[:L]
    np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
    c=0.5*(c+c[::-1])
    np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
    return c
```
Sym after maximum? If sym creates negative? maybe, we can maximum after sym. Use `c += c[::-1]; c*=0.5; np.maximum(c,0,out=c)`. Good.

```

`_grad_h(a,p)`:
```
def _grad_h(a,p):
   m=a.size; ctx=_ctx(m); f=ctx['fft']; L=ctx['outlen']
   pr=np.zeros(f)
   pr[:L]=p[::-1]
   Fx=np.fft.rfft(a,f); Fp=np.fft.rfft(pr,f)
   conv=np.fft.irfft(Fx*Fp,f)
   h=conv[m-1:L][::-1]  # L=2m-1, slice m-1:L => elements m-1..2m-2 length m
   # sym? h maybe. return h
   return h
```
Slice `conv[m-1:L]` where L=2m-1; length L-(m-1)=m. Good.

`_pos_norm(a, floor=None)`:
```
def _pos_norm(a, floor=None):
    a=np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    if floor is None: floor=1e-12/max(a.size,1)
    a=a+floor
    s=a.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        a=np.ones(a.size, dtype=float); s=a.size
    return a/s
```
For final best, _pos_norm adds floor which changes sequence slightly but fine. But if parent already sum1 and nonnegative, adding 1e-12 negligible. For low final candidate, return _pos_norm? We can return normalized without floor for exact? Sum >0. Could use `_normalize_no_floor` for final: clip, normalize if sum>0. But floor helps. It may add zeros to 1e-12; fine. Sum still 1? after normalize. Values positive.

`_softmax(z)`:

def _softmax(z):
   z=np.asarray(z,float)
   z=z - np.nanmax(z)? If no finite fallback.
   z=z - np.max(z)
   e=np.exp(z)
   s=e.sum()
   if not finite/s<=0: return ones(m)/m
   return e/s
```
But if z has nan, fallback. Use finite handling.

z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300))

Implementation:
```
def _refine(a0, time_limit, maxfun=200, stage_fracs=(...), ridge=1e-10):
    a0 = np.asarray(a0,float).ravel()
    m=a0.size
    if m<1: return np.array([1.0]), 2.0
    t_start=time.monotonic()
    a=_pos_norm(a0, 1e-12/m)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_s=_score(a)
    z=_logsafe(a)
    bounds=[(-60.0,60.0)]*m
    for frac in stage_fracs:
       if time.monotonic() - t_start >= time_limit: break
       if time.monotonic() >= _deadline - 60? We can pass global? Better receive `deadline` and check.
       c=_conv(a); mc=float(c.max())
       tau=max(mc*frac, 1e-12*max(mc,1e-12))
       # maybe ensure tau > 0
       def fun(z, tau=tau, m=m):
           # check time? cannot inside minimize.
           zc = z - z.mean()
           if not np.all(np.isfinite(zc)):
               return 1e100, np.zeros(m)
           # bounded? L-BFGS bounds, but just in case clip for exp
           zexp = zc - np.max(zc)
           e = np.exp(zexp)
           s = e.sum()
           if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
               aa = np.ones(m)/m
           else:
               aa = e/s
           c = _conv(aa)
           cmax = float(c.max())
           if not np.isfinite(cmax):
               return 1e100, np.zeros(m)
           sh = (c - cmax)/tau
           # Since sh<=0, exp safe; but due to roundoff maybe tiny positive? use np.minimum(sh,0)
           sh = np.where(sh < 0.0, sh, 0.0)  # ensures no >0? Actually max exactly 0 after cmax; c-cmax <= 0.
           ew = np.exp(sh)
           Z = ew.sum()
           if not np.isfinite(Z) or Z <= 0:
               # fallback
              p = (c >= cmax - 1e-15*max(1,cmax)).astype(float)
               p /= max(p.sum(),1)
               logZ = 0.0
           else:
               p = ew / Z
               logZ = float(np.log(Z))
           val = cmax + tau*logZ + ridge*np.dot(zc,zc)
           # sym p for gradient
           p = 0.5*(p + p[::-1]); p /= p.sum()
           h = _grad_h(aa, p)
           grad_a = (2.0/tau)*h
           dot = float(np.dot(aa, grad_a))
           gz = aa*grad_a - aa*dot + 2.0*ridge*zc
           if not np.all(np.isfinite(gz)):
               gz = np.zeros(m)
           gz = gz - gz.mean()
           return val, gz
       try:
           res = minimize(fun, z, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=False,
                          bounds=bounds,
                          options={'maxiter': 25, 'maxfun': maxfun, 'ftol': 1e-10, 'gtol': 1e-5, 'maxls': 30})
           if hasattr(res,'x') and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
               z = res.x - np.mean(res.x)
           else:
               break
           a = _softmax(z)
           s = _score(a)
           if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
               best_s=s; best_a=a.copy()
       except Exception:
           break
       # Check wall
       if time.monotonic() - t_start >= time_limit: break
    return best_a, best_s
```

```
def _add_smooth_noise(a, rng, rel_amp=0.05):
    n=len(a)
    z = _rand_lowpass_as_array(n, rng, kmax=4)
    z = z - z.mean()
    # scale so max |z| ~ rel_amp / max(a)? We want change coefficients by ~5%
    sdev = z.std()
    if sdev>0: z = z/sdev
    # a mean ~1/n; want absolute noise ~ rel_amp*mean? rel_amp=0.2 -> 0.2/n
    a2 = a + rel_amp * a.mean() * z
    return _pos_norm(a2)
```

Let's implement `_refine` with time limit but no global deadline. We will call when enough time. It should not exceed time_limit significantly due maxfun. It records local best. It may also return `None` if no improvement? We'll return a,s.

```
def _refine(a0, time_limit, maxfun=200, stage_fracs=(0.35,0.18,0.09,0.045,0.02,0.01)):
    a0 = np.asarray(a0, dtype=float).ravel()
    m = int(a0.size)
    if m <= 0:
       a=np.array([1.0]); return a, 2.0
    if time_limit <=0: return _pos_norm(a0), _score(...)
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    a = _pos_norm(a0, 1e-10/m)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_s=_score(a)
    z = _logsafe(a)
    ctx = _ctx(m); f_len=ctx['fft']; L=ctx['outlen']
    bounds = [(-80.0, 80.0)]*m
    for frac in stage_fracs:
       if time.monotonic()-t_start >= time_limit: break
       # Estimate maxfun for stage based on remaining time? Currently fixed.
       c0 = _conv(a); mc=float(c0.max())
       if mc <=0: mc=1e-12
       tau = max(mc*frac, 1e-12*mc)
       def fun(z):
          zc = z - z.mean()
          if not np.all(np.isfinite(zc)):
             return 1e100, np.zeros(m)
          zexp = zc - zc.max()
          ee = np.exp(zexp)
          sd = ee.sum()
          if not np.isfinite(sd) or sd <=0:
             aa = np.ones(m)/m
          else:
             aa = ee/sd
          F = np.fft.rfft(aa, f_len)
          c = np.fft.irfft(F*F, f_len)
          c = c[:L]
          c += c[::-1]; c *=0.5
          np.maximum(c, 0, out=c)
          mc = float(c.max())
          if not np.isfinite(mc):
             return 1e100, np.zeros(m)
          sh = (c - mc)/tau
          # clip tiny positive:
          if sh[0] > 0: sh = np.minimum(sh, 0.0) # sh array
          ew = np.exp(sh)
          Z = float(ew.sum())
          if not np.isfinite(Z) or Z <= 0:
              active = (c >= mc - 1e-14*max(1.0,mc)).astype(float)
              sact=active.sum()
              if sact <=0: active = np.ones(L)/L
              else: active /= sact
              Z=1.0
              p=active
          else:
              p=ew/Z
              logZ=np.log(Z)
           p = 0.5*(p + p[::-1]); p /= p.sum()
           pr = np.zeros(f_len, dtype=float)
           pr[:L] = p[::-1]
           Fp = np.fft.rfft(pr, f_len)
           ch = np.fft.irfft(F*Fp, f_len)
           h = ch[m-1:L][::-1]
           grad_a = (2.0/tau) * h
           dot = float(np.dot(aa, grad_a))
           gz = aa * grad_a - aa * dot
           if not np.all(np.isfinite(gz)):
              gz = np.zeros(m)
           gz -= gz.mean()
           val = mc + tau*float(np.log(Z))
           return val, gz
       try:
          res = minimize(fun, z, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, bounds=bounds,
                         options={'maxiter':30, 'maxfun':maxfun, 'ftol':1e-11, 'gtol':1e-5, 'maxls':30})
          if getattr(res, 'x', None) is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
             z = res.x - res.x.mean()
             a = _softmax(z)
             s = _score(a)
             if np.isfinite(s) and s < best_s:
                best_s=s; best_a=a.copy()
          # even if res not success, a may changed? no
       except Exception:
          pass
    return best_a, best_s
```

   active=...

else:

```

`_downsample`: Need if m > len? call upsample. But for parent low, m<. Implementation:
```
def _downsample(a,m):
  a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
  n=a.size
  if n == m: return a.copy()
  if n < m: return _upsample(a,m)
  idx = (np.arange(n) * m // n).astype(np.int64)
  idx[idx >= m] = m-1
  sums = np.bincount(idx, weights=a, minlength=m)
  counts = np.bincount(idx, minlength=m).astype(float)
  out = sums / np.maximum(counts,1.0)
  return _pos_norm(out, 1e-12/m)
```
If parent sum 1, average masses okay. If some block no samples, out zero; _pos_norm floors.

`_upsample`: If m>1 use np.interp. For m=1, repeat? Not. If a is scalar? no.
```
def _upsample(a,n):
  a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel(); m=a.size
  if m == n: return a.copy()
  if m == 1: out = np.full(n, a[0] if a.size else 1.0)
  elif m < n:
     xo=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m); xn=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
     out=np.interp(xn,xo,a)
  else:
     return _downsample(a,n)
  return _pos_norm(out,1e-12/n)
```
If n=1? not.

`_rand_smooth`:
```
def _rand_smooth(m, rng, kmax=None):
  if kmax is None: kmax=max(2, min(8, m//4))
  F=np.zeros(m//2+1, dtype=complex)
  n_freq=min(4,kmax)
  freqs=rng.choice(np.arange(1,kmax+1), size=n_freq, replace=False)
  for k in freqs:
     amp=rng.uniform(0.4,1.0)
     ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
     v=amp*np.exp(1j*ph)
     F[k]=v
     mr=m-k
     if 0 <= mr < len(F): F[mr]=np.conj(v)
  x=np.fft.irfft(F, m)
  x=x-x.mean()
  sd=float(x.std())
  if sd > 1e-12: x=x/sd
  amp=rng.uniform(0.3,0.9)
  out=np.exp(amp*x)
  return _pos_norm(out, 1e-10/m)
```
This gives smooth random.

return _pos_norm(np.maximum(out,1e-6), 1e-10/m)
```

Full refinement loop:
```
full_list = [( _score(a), i, a) for i,a in enumerate(full_arrays)]
full_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
full_list = full_list[:min(len, 9)]
full_deadline = deadline - 60
for idx, (s0, _, a0) in enumerate(full_list):
    if time.monotonic() >= full_deadline - 20: break
    rem = full_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem < 25: break

```python
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_CTX_CACHE = {}


def _ctx(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m in _CTX_CACHE:
        return _CTX_CACHE[m]
    need = 2 * m - 1
    p = 1
    while p < need:
        p <<= 1
    ctx = {"m": m, "fft": p, "outlen": need}
    _CTX_CACHE[m] = ctx
    return ctx


def _conv(a):
    m = int(a.size)
    ctx = _ctx(m)
    f = ctx["fft"]
    L = ctx["outlen"]
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, f)
    c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, f)
    c = c[:L]
    c += c[::-1]
    c *= 0.5
    np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
    return c


def _score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * float(_conv(a).max()))


def _pos_norm(a, floor=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=float)
    if floor is None:
        floor = 1e-12 / max(1, a.size)
    a = a + floor
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=float)
        s = float(a.size)
    return a / s


def _softmax(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float).ravel()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=float) / max(1, z.size)
    z = z - z.max()
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=float) / max(1, z.size)
    return e / s


def _logsafe(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-300)
    z = np.log(a)
    z = z - z.mean()
    return z


def _downsample(a, m):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=float)
    if n == m:
        return _pos_norm(a, 1e-12 / m)
    if n < m:
        return _upsample(a, m)
    idx = (np.arange(n) * m // n).astype(np.int64)
    np.minimum(idx, m - 1, out=idx)
    sums = np.bincount(idx, weights=a, minlength=m)
    counts = np.bincount(idx, minlength=m).astype(float)
    out = sums / np.maximum(counts, 1.0)
    return _pos_norm(out, 1e-12 / m)


def _upsample(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    m = int(a.size)
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=float)
    if m == n:
        return _pos_norm(a, 1e-12 / n)
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(a[0]), dtype=float)
    if m < n:
        xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
        xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        out = np.interp(xn, xo, a)
    else:
        return _downsample(a, n)
    return _pos_norm(out, 1e-12 / n)


def _raw_lowpass(m, rng, kmax=None, nfreq=4):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
    if kmax is None:
        kmax = max(2, min(8, m // 4))
    half = m // 2 + 1
    F = np.zeros(half, dtype=np.complex128)
    kmax = min(int(kmax), half - 1)
    nfreq = max(1, min(int(nfreq), kmax))
    freqs = rng.choice(np.arange(1, kmax + 1), size=nfreq, replace=False)
    for k in freqs:
        amp = rng.uniform(0.35, 1.0)
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        v = amp * np.exp(1j * ph)
        F[k] = v
        mr = m - int(k)
        if 0 <= mr < half:
            F[mr] = np.conj(v)
    x = np.fft.irfft(F, m)
    x = x - float(x.mean())
    sd = float(x.std())
    if sd > 1e-14:
        x *= 1.0 / sd
    return x


def _rand_smooth(m, rng):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=float) / max(1, m)
    x = _raw_lowpass(m, rng)
    amp = rng.uniform(0.3, 0.95)
    out = np.exp(amp * x)
    return _pos_norm(out, 1e-10 / m)


def _linear(m, s):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=float) / max(1, m)
    u = np.linspace(-1.0, 1.0, m)
    out = 1.0 + float(s) * u
    out = np.maximum(out, 1e-6)
    return _pos_norm(out, 1e-10 / m)


def _two_block(m, rng):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=float) / max(1, m)
    p = rng.uniform(0.30, 0.70)
    alpha = rng.uniform(0.25, 0.75)
    k = int(round(p * m))
    if k <= 0:
        k = 1
    if k >= m:
        k = m - 1
    a = np.empty(m, dtype=float)
    a[:k] = float(alpha) / float(k)
    a[k:] = (1.0 - float(alpha)) / float(m - k)
    return _pos_norm(a, 1e-10 / m)


def _add_smooth_noise(a, rng, rel_amp):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 1:
        return _pos_norm(a, 1e-12 / max(1, n))
    x = _raw_lowpass(n, rng)
    sd = float(x.std())
    if sd > 1e-14:
        x *= 1.0 / sd
    b = a + float(rel_amp) * float(a.mean()) * x
    return _pos_norm(b, 1e-10 / n)


def _maxfun_for(m, time_limit):
    m = int(m)
    tl = max(0.0, float(time_limit))
    if tl <= 0.0:
        return 50
    if m <= 512:
        return int(max(120, min(800, tl * 150.0)))
    if m <= 2048:
        return int(max(150, min(600, tl * 60.0)))
    return int(max(100, min(300, tl * 20.0)))


def _refine(a0, time_limit, stage_fracs=None):
    a0 = np.asarray(a0, dtype=float).ravel()
    m = int(a0.size)
    if m <= 0:
        a = np.array([1.0], dtype=float)
        return a, 2.0
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    if m == 1:
        a = np.array([1.0], dtype=float)
        return a, 2.0
    if time_limit is None or time_limit <= 0.0:
        a = _pos_norm(a0, 1e-10 / m)
        return a, _score(a)

    if stage_fracs is None:
        stage_fracs = (0.35, 0.18, 0.09, 0.045, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005)
    maxfun = _maxfun_for(m, time_limit)

    a = _pos_norm(a0, 1e-10 / m)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_s = _score(a)
    z = _logsafe(a)

    ctx = _ctx(m)
    f_len = ctx["fft"]
    L = ctx["outlen"]
    bounds = [(-80.0, 80.0)] * m

    for frac in stage_fracs:
        if time.monotonic() - t_start >= time_limit:
            break
        c0 = _conv(a)
        mc0 = float(c0.max())
        if not np.isfinite(mc0) or mc0 <= 0.0:
            mc0 = 1.0 / max(1, m)
        tau = max(float(mc0) * float(frac), 1e-12 * float(mc0))

        def fun(z, tau=tau, m=m, f_len=f_len, L=L):
            zc = z - z.mean()
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(zc)):
                return 1e100, np.zeros(m, dtype=float)

            zexp = zc - zc.max()
            ee = np.exp(zexp)
            sd = float(ee.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(sd) or sd <= 0.0:
                aa = np.ones(m, dtype=float) / float(m)
            else:
                aa = ee / sd

            F = np.fft.rfft(aa, f_len)
            c = np.fft.irfft(F * F, f_len)
            c = c[:L]
            c += c[::-1]
            c *= 0.5
            np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
            mc = float(c.max())
            if not np.isfinite(mc):
                return 1e100, np.zeros(m, dtype=float)

            sh = (c - mc) / tau
            sh = np.minimum(sh, 0.0)
            ew = np.exp(sh)
            Z = float(ew.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(Z) or Z <= 0.0:
                active = (c >= mc - 1e-9 * max(1.0, mc)).astype(float)
                sa = float(active.sum())
                if sa <= 0.0:
                    active = np.ones(L, dtype=float) / float(L)
                else:
                    active /= sa
                p = active
                logZ = 0.0
            else:
                p = ew / Z
                logZ = float(np.log(Z))

            p = 0.5 * (p + p[::-1])
            ps = float(p.sum())
            if ps > 0.0 and np.isfinite(ps):
                p /= ps
            else:
                p = np.ones(L, dtype=float) / float(L)

            pr = np.zeros(f_len, dtype=float)
            pr[:L] = p[::-1]
            Fp = np.fft.rfft(pr, f_len)
            ch = np.fft.irfft(F * Fp, f_len)
            h = ch[m - 1:L][::-1]

            grad_a = (2.0 / tau) * h
            dot = float(np.dot(aa, grad_a))
            gz = aa * grad_a - aa * dot
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(gz)):
                gz = np.zeros(m, dtype=float)
            gz -= gz.mean()

            val = mc + tau * logZ
            return float(val), gz

        try:
            res = minimize(
                fun,
                z,
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                bounds=bounds,
                options={
                    "maxiter": 30,
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "ftol": 1e-11,
                    "gtol": 1e-5,
                    "maxls": 30,
                },
            )
            if getattr(res, "x", None) is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
                z = res.x - res.x.mean()
                a_now = _softmax(z)
                s_now = _score(a_now)
                if np.isfinite(s_now) and s_now < best_s:
                    best_s = float(s_now)
                    best_a = a_now.copy()
                a = a_now
            else:
                break
        except Exception:
            break

        if time.monotonic() - t_start >= time_limit:
            break

    return best_a, best_s


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1300.0
    reserve = 90.0
    deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget - reserve)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    parent = None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=float).ravel()
        p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        ps = float(p.sum())
        if p.size > 0 and ps > 0.01:
            p = p / ps
            if p.size > 10000:
                p = _downsample(p, 8192)
            parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        full_N = int(parent.size)
        start_parent = _pos_norm(parent, 1e-12 / full_N)
    else:
        full_N = 4096
        start_parent = np.ones(full_N, dtype=float) / float(full_N)

    pool = []
    best_a = start_parent.copy()
    best_s = _score(best_a)

    def _add_pool(a, s=None):
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
        if a.size == 0:
            return
        if s is None or not np.isfinite(s):
            s = _score(a)
        if np.isfinite(s):
            pool.append((float(s), a.copy()))

    _add_pool(best_a, best_s)

    # Low-resolution exploration to find good low-dimensional shapes.
    low_pool = []
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 90.0:
        m_list = [256, 512, 1024, 2048]
        for m in m_list:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline - 100.0:
                break
            starts = []
            if parent is not None:
                starts.append(_downsample(parent, m))
            starts.append(_two_block(m, rng))
            starts.append(_linear(m, rng.uniform(0.08, 0.35) * (1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0)))
            for _ in range(3):
                starts.append(_rand_smooth(m, rng))

            for a0 in starts:
                now = time.monotonic()
                if now >= deadline - 100.0:
                    break
                tl = min(10.0, max(2.0, 0.25 * (deadline - 100.0 - now)))
                if tl < 1.5:
                    continue
                try:
                    a_low, s_low = _refine(
                        a0,
                        tl,
                        stage_fracs=(0.45, 0.25, 0.12, 0.06, 0.03, 0.015),
                    )
                except Exception:
                    continue
                if a_low is not None and a_low.size == m:
                    low_pool.append((float(s_low), a_low.copy()))
                    _add_pool(a_low, s_low)
                    if np.isfinite(s_low) and s_low < best_s:
                        best_s = float(s_low)
                        best_a = a_low.copy()

    low_pool.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])

    full_starts = []

    def _add_full(a, priority=0):
        a = _pos_norm(a, 1e-12 / full_N)
        if a.size != full_N:
            a = _upsample(a, full_N)
            a = _pos_norm(a, 1e-12 / full_N)
        s = _score(a)
        full_starts.append((priority, float(s), a))
        _add_pool(a, s)
        return s

    if parent is not None:
        _add_full(start_parent, priority=0)
        blur_m = max(64, min(512, full_N // 8))
        _add_full(_upsample(_downsample(start_parent, blur_m), full_N), priority=1)
        _add_full(_add_smooth_noise(start_parent, rng, 0.18), priority=2)

    # Best low-resolution shapes, upsampled to the working length.
    low_top = []
    for s_low, a_low in low_pool[:4]:
        try:
            a_full = _upsample(a_low, full_N)
        except Exception:
            continue
        s_full = _score(a_full)
        low_top.append((float(s_full), a_full))
        if len(low_top) >= 3:
            break

    for i, (s_full, a_full) in enumerate(low_top):
        _add_full(a_full, priority=1 + i)

    _add_full(np.ones(full_N, dtype=float) / float(full_N), priority=5)
    _add_full(_linear(full_N, 0.20), priority=6)
    _add_full(_linear(full_N, -0.20), priority=6)
    _add_full(_two_block(full_N, rng), priority=7)
    _add_full(_rand_smooth(full_N, rng), priority=8)
    _add_full(_rand_smooth(full_N, rng), priority=8)

    # Remove obvious duplicates by initial score: keep at most 10 full starts.
    full_starts.sort(key=lambda x: (x[0], x[1]))
    if len(full_starts) > 10:
        # Preserve the first few priority entries even if their score is not best.
        kept = []
        seen = set()
        for pr, s, a in full_starts:
            if pr < 4 and len([k for k in kept if k[0] < 4]) < 4:
                kept.append((pr, s, a))
                seen.add(id(a))
        for pr, s, a in full_starts:
            if len(kept) >= 10:
                break
            if id(a) in seen:
                continue
            kept.append((pr, s, a))
            seen.add(id(a))
        full_starts = kept[:10]

    full_deadline = deadline - 70.0
    for idx, (_, s0, a0) in enumerate(full_starts):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= full_deadline - 25.0:
            break
        rem = full_deadline - now
        if rem < 25.0:
            break
        remaining = max(1, len(full_starts) - idx)
        tl = min(120.0, max(8.0, rem / float(remaining)))
        try:
            a_full, s_full = _refine(
                a0,
                tl,
                stage_fracs=(0.30, 0.15, 0.08, 0.04, 0.02, 0.01, 0.005),
            )
        except Exception:
            continue
        a_full = _pos_norm(a_full, 1e-12 / full_N)
        s_full = _score(a_full)
        _add_pool(a_full, s_full)
        if np.isfinite(s_full) and s_full < best_s:
            best_s = float(s_full)
            best_a = a_full.copy()

    # Final polishing of the current best, plus a few smooth perturbations.
    final_deadline = deadline - 45.0
    perturbs = 0
    while time.monotonic() < final_deadline - 30.0 and perturbs < 4:
        now = time.monotonic()
        rem = final_deadline - now
        if rem < 35.0:
            break
        if perturbs == 0:
            a0 = best_a.copy()
            tl = min(90.0, 0.6 * rem)
            fracs = (0.12, 0.06, 0.03, 0.015, 0.008, 0.005)
        else:
            a0 = _add_smooth_noise(best_a, rng, 0.04 + 0.06 * float(perturbs))
            tl = min(50.0, 0.35 * rem)
            fracs = (0.20, 0.10, 0.05, 0.025)
        try:
            a_try, s_try = _refine(a0, tl, stage_fracs=fracs)
        except Exception:
            break
        a_try = _pos_norm(a_try, 1e-12 / int(a_try.size))
        s_try = _score(a_try)
        _add_pool(a_try, s_try)
        if np.isfinite(s_try) and s_try < best_s:
            best_s = float(s_try)
            best_a = a_try.copy()
        perturbs += 1

    # Validate top candidates using the supplied exact evaluator.
    exact_best_a = best_a.copy()
    exact_best_s = float("inf")

    def _try_exact(a):
        nonlocal exact_best_a, exact_best_s
        if a is None or a.size == 0:
            return
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=float).ravel()
        if a.size > 20000:
            return
        try:
            s = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a]))
        except Exception:
            return
        if np.isfinite(s) and s < exact_best_s:
            exact_best_s = float(s)
            exact_best_a = a.copy()

    _try_exact(exact_best_a)

    # A few of the strongest surrogate-score candidates too.
    pool_sorted = sorted(pool, key=lambda x: x[0])
    for s, a in pool_sorted[:6]:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 18.0:
            break
        _try_exact(a)

    # Last-resort validation/fallback.
    if exact_best_a is None or exact_best_a.size == 0 or exact_best_s == float("inf"):
        exact_best_a = best_a
    try:
        s_final = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in exact_best_a]))
        if not np.isfinite(s_final):
            exact_best_a = None
    except Exception:
        exact_best_a = None

    if exact_best_a is None or exact_best_a.size == 0:
        exact_best_a = np.ones(64, dtype=float) / 64.0
    else:
        exact_best_a = np.asarray(exact_best_a, dtype=float).ravel()
        exact_best_a = np.where(np.isfinite(exact_best_a), exact_best_a, 0.0)
        exact_best_a = np.clip(exact_best_a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        ssum = float(exact_best_a.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ssum) or ssum <= 0.01:
            exact_best_a = np.ones(64, dtype=float) / 64.0

    return [float(x) for x in exact_best_a]
```
